Term for output p (shift k = p-(n-1)):

Alternative objective via integer counts and update incrementally for swap. For binary swap, we can update all t_k counts in O(n) (since each bit affects terms for all offsets), enabling millions iterations. Let's derive incremental update.

Given current h (0/1) length n, weight W=n/2. Let c[k] for k=-(n-1)..(n-1) = np.correlate(h,1-h) count. We want max. For binary, c[k] can be updated when flipping a pair a (1->0), b (0->1). Equivalent adding Δh: -1 at a, +1 at b. c is bilinear in h and 1-h. Update for all k via contributions involving positions a and b.

Let's derive from s or direct. c[k] = ∑_i h_{i+k}(1-h_i) for k index with valid i (0 <= i < n, 0<=i+k<n). For k can negative. Let's define offset d = k (shift output n-1+d): c[d]=∑_{i=max(0,-d)}^{min(n-1,n-1-d)} h_{i+d}(1-h_i). L=n-|d|.

If change h at indices p (δ=-1) and q (δ=+1). Delta c[d] = sum over i where terms involve p or q:
Term = h_{i+d} - h_i h_{i+d}.
Change δ_p,δ_q affects linear part and products.
General: Δc[d] = ∑_i δ_{i+d} (1-h_i^{new? or old? need consistent})? Bilinear uses final? If we use old h, exact change:
new product h_i+h_iδ_i, h_{i+d}+δ_{i+d}. new term = (h_i+δ_i)(1 - h_{i+d} - δ_{i+d})? Wait 1-h_shift new = 1 - h_{i+d} - δ_{i+d}. Product = h_i(1-hshift) + δ_i(1-h) - h_i δ_shift - (1-h)δ_shift? Let's do: 
old = h_i(1-hj), j=i+d.
new=(h_i+δ_i)(1 - h_j - δ_j) = h_i - h_i h_j + δ_i - δ_i h_j - h_i δ_j - δ_i δ_j.
Delta = δ_i(1-h_j) - h_i δ_j - δ_i δ_j (using old values). If δ_i,δ_j known.
For our changes only at p,q. Thus for each d, contributions:
For i such that i=p (δ_i=δ_p): term δ_p(1-h_j) where j=p+d.
For i such that j=q (i+d=q, i=q-d, δ_j=δ_q): term -h_i δ_q.
For i such that both i=p and j=q? i=d? when d=q-p: δ_i=δ_p, δ_j=δ_q: the formula includes δ_i(1-h_j)-h_iδ_j-δ_iδ_j = δ_p(1-h_q)-h_pδ_q-δ_pδ_q. Also for i such that i=q and j=p (d=p-q): contributions same. But if we sum over i with δ_i and i with δ_j separately, need avoid double count and delta_ij.
Simpler: Δc[d] = ∑_{i} [δ_i (1-h_{i+d}^{old}) - h_i^{old} δ_{i+d}^{old} - δ_i δ_{i+d}^{old}], with δ nonzero only p,q. So:
Contribution from i=p if 0<=p+d<n: δ_p (1 - h_{p+d})
Contribution from i=q if 0<=q+d<n: δ_q (1 - h_{q+d})
Contribution from j=p => i=p-d if valid: -h_{p-d} δ_p
Contribution from j=q => i=q-d: -h_{q-d} δ_q
If i=p and j=q simultaneously (d=q-p): then the i=p contribution and j=q contribution both included, and need subtract δ_p δ_q? Wait formula includes -δ_iδ_j for same i where both δ nonzero. For i=p,j=q (d=q-p): contributions from δ_i (i=p) + (-h_i δ_j) = δ_p(1-h_q) - h_p δ_q. Formula exact = δ_p(1-h_q)-h_pδ_q-δ_pδ_q. So add -δ_pδ_q. Similarly i=q,j=p (d=p-q): add -δ_qδ_p.
So update can be O(n) by adding arrays for each changed position:
For r with δ:
 For all d such that r+d in [0,n): add δ*(1 - h[r+d]) to c[d] (with d = j-r, j other index)
 For all d such that r-d in [0,n): add -h[r-d]*δ to c[d] (d = r-i)
Then for both positions, adjust overlap if d=q-p and d=p-q subtract δ_pδ_q for those d. But careful: If we sum both r=p and r=q, for d=q-p, contributions: from r=p as i: δ_p(1-h_q); from r=q as j: -h_p δ_q (since r-d=p? r=q,d=q-p => i=p). They match formula without -δ_pδ_q. So add -δ_pδ_q at d=q-p. Similarly d=p-q: from r=q as i: δ_q(1-h_p); from r=p as j: -h_q δ_p; add -δ_qδ_p. But δ=-1,+1, product -1, so subtract -1 => +1? Need exact test.
Alternatively derive using s maybe simpler with undirected. But we can implement full update to verify mentally.

That's same as np.correlate(h,1-h,full)[n-1+d]? np.correlate(h,g) c_full[m] = ∑_i h[i+m?]. Let's check quick known: np.correlate([0,1,2],[1,1,0]) full? Candidate evaluation pseudocode:
def try_swap(state,p,q):
  h=state.h; c=state.c
  c += calc_delta(..., h) # modifies c
  newmax = c.max()
  if accept:
     h[p]=0; h[q]=1
  else:
     c -= calc_delta(..., h) # but h unchanged, same delta; can store delta array? To avoid recompute, compute delta first and apply.
  return newmax

Let's design code.

Key components:
- Top-level helper functions, no lambdas/closures.
- run(seed, budget_s) returns best (h_values, c5_bound, n_points). h_values as np.ndarray float maybe length n. If binary, float 0/1.
- Use initial_h_values if in globals. It is length 80, presumably continuous. We must not assign to it. Use np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).Copy. For binary initial, choose top n/2 values by rank.
- We need initial construction if no parent: algorithmic. Examples:
   * Random binary balanced with seed.
   * "block" sequence: alternate blocks of length chosen by random, fill to n, adjust weight.
   * Low-pass sign: generate frequencies, inverse random, threshold to binary balanced. Or use np.fft of random lowpass: create random phases for first few frequencies, real signal, threshold median.
   * Use initial parent projected to smaller/larger n: interpolate parent to n (maybe central limit), threshold.

- Objective evaluation: For continuous candidate, c = correlate(h,1-h) using numpy. We can implement our own correlate to avoid sign concerns. `def eval_c5(h): dx=2.0/len(h); c = overlapping_corr(h,1-h); return float(np.max(c)*dx)`. But for speed local use own c.

Let's implement `corr_shifts(h)` returning c array length 2n-1 with d index offset n-1 matching our definition: c[offset] = sum h_i(1-h_i) (for d=0). For d>0, sum h[i+d]*(1-h[i]); d<0 sum h[i+d]*(1-h[i]). For continuous. For binary, c0=0.
Vectorized O(n^2):
```
def corr_shifts(h):
    n=len(h); out=np.zeros(2*n-1,float)
    out[n-1]=np.sum(h*(1-h))
    for d in range(1,n):
        out[n-1+d] = np.dot(h[d:], 1-h[:-d])
        out[n-1-d] = np.dot(h[:-d], 1-h[d:])
    return out
```
For n up to 300 maybe loop 300*dot small. Could use FFT for initial evaluations but local not.

Thus update rules:
For r change δ:
- As shifted: d = r - i for i=0..n-1, i≠r => d range [r-n+1, r], c[idx] += δ * (1 - h[i]) where i = r - d.
- As original: d = b - r for b=0..n-1, b≠r => d range [-r, n-1-r], c[idx] += -h[b] δ where b = r + d.
Pair corrections: d = q-p (a=p,b=q) and d = p-q (a=q,b=p): c[d] += -δpδq.

Now vector arrays:
For r:
  shifted: i from 0..n-1 (exclude r), d = r - i. idx = offset + r - i; val = δ*(1 - h[i])
  original: b from 0..n-1 (exclude r), d = b - r. idx = offset + b - r; val = -δ*h[b]
Pair corr for d=q-p: add -δpδq; d=p-q add same.

For binary swap δp=-1,δq=+1 product -1, correction +1 both.

Could generalize for accepted? δp always -1,δq +1 because swapping one to zero q. So delta formula can be simplified no δ variables:
For p (one ->0):
- as shifted: δ=-1: subtract (1 - h[i]) from c[shift_idx]
- as original: -δ*h = +h[b]: add h[b] to c[orig_idx]
For q (zero ->1):
- as shifted: add (1 - h[i])
- as original: subtract h[b]
Corrections p,q: +1 at both cross d.
Let's verify with n=2 old [1,0] p=0,q=1:
p shifted: i? r=0 shifted d range [0? exclude], i=1? d= -? Corrections +1 at d=1 and -1. Totals d1:0+1=+1; d-1:-1-1+1=-1. correct.
Good.
So implement specialized:

delta[shift_idx[q]] += (1 - h[shift_other[q]])
delta[orig_idx[q]] -= h[orig_other[q]]
delta[off+q-p] += 1
delta[off+p-q] += 1
```
Where arrays exclude self. Values h uint8; operations produce int arrays. `1 - h[indices]` yields int? h uint8, 1-h gives int? Actually numpy uint8 subtract from int gives int. Good. Starting from best binary h as float. Need maintain sum exactly n/2. Use soft objective. We can use projected gradient on h with log-sum-exp.

```
def soft_objective(h, beta):
   c = corr_shifts(h)
   # max of c*? maximize soft max of c (not multiplied dx since constant)
   m = c.max()
   return (1/beta)* (m + np.log(np.exp(beta*(c-m)).sum()))
```
Gradient: weights = exp(beta*(c-m))/Z (or exp beta c / sum). Need ∂c_d. We can compute d = idx - off. For each d where weight > eps, add w*grad_d. Complexity O(n * active). But c len ~2n. For n=160, O50k per iteration; fine. We can compute gradient more efficiently by using relationship with correlation? Let's derive direct loops for continuous gradient of soft max:
For each d, grad_d[j] = (1-h[j-d]) if j-d valid - h[j+d] if j+d valid. We can accumulate:
Initialize grad zeros.
For d>0: valid i=0..L-1, original i, shifted i+d.
 grad[0:L] -= w * h[d:d+L]
 grad[d:n] += w * (1 - h[:L])
For d<0: d=-m, valid i=m..n-1? term h[i-m](1-h_i), original i, shifted i-m.
 valid length L=n-m; original i=m..n-1, shifted 0..L-1.
 grad[m:n] -= w * h[:L]
 grad[0:L] += w * (1 - h[m:n])
For d=0: grad[:]-=2w*h? c0=∑h(1-h), derivative 1-2h.
This is vectorized loops over d (<=n). Good.

def binary_local_search(n, h0, time_limit, rng, pre, max_incr=3):
    t_end = time.time() + time_limit
    h = np.array(h0, dtype=np.uint8).copy()
    if h.sum() != n//2: adjust swaps random until correct.
    c = np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.int64)
    # compute c initial by summing changes? Use corr_shifts then convert? Need c corresponds our d. corr_shifts returns int? h float? Use binary direct:
    for d in range(1,n): ...
    off = n-1
    # build lists
    ones = [i for i in range(n) if h[i]==1]
    zeros = [i for i in range(n) if h[i]==0]
    pos1 = np.full(n,-1,np.int64); pos0...
    for idx,i in enumerate(ones): pos1[i]=idx ...
    curM = int(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); bestM=curM
    delta = np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.int64)
    worst = np.flatnonzero(c >= curM - 1)
    count=0
    no_improve=0
    while time.time()<t_end:
       count +=1
       if count % 256 ==0: recalc worst
       # candidate choice
       mode = rng.random()
       p=-1; q=-1
       if mode<0.45 and len(worst)>0:
          wi = int(worst[rng.integers(len(worst))]); d=wi-off
          # sample contribution p,q direct with attempts
          if d>0:
             L=n-d
             for _ in range(8):
                i=int(rng.integers(L)); p=i+d; q=i; if h[p]==1 and h[q]==0: break
          else:
             m=-d; L=n-m
             for ... i, p=i; q=i+m; if h[p]==1 and h[q]==0
          if p<0: fallback random
       elif mode<0.6:
          # repair one side
          choose worst d, sample p one; q=random zero (or sample q zero and p=random one?)
       else:
          p=random ones; q=random zeros
       # compute delta and trial
       delta.fill(0)
       # add p contributions (one->0)
       si=pre['shift_idx'][p]; so=pre['shift_other'][p]
       delta[si] -= (1 - h[so])  # h uint8, 1 - ... int
       oi=pre['orig_idx'][p]; oo=pre['orig_other'][p]
       delta[oi] += h[oo]
       # q zero->1
       si=...
       delta[si] += (1 - h[so])
       delta[oi] -= h[oo]
       delta[off+q-p] += 1
       delta[off+p-q] += 1
       c += delta
       newM = int(c.max())
       accept=False
       if newM < curM:
          accept=True; no_improve=0
       elif newM == curM:
          # accept equal sometimes; maybe if rng.random()<0.5 or to allow plateau
          accept=(rng.random()<0.5)
       else:
          incr = newM - curM
          if incr <= max_incr:
             # temperature based on time/no_improve
             T = max(0.1, max_incr - 0.3*incr?)
             if rng.random() < math.exp(-incr / max(0.1, 1.0 + no_improve*0.01?)):
                accept=True
       if accept:
          # update h/lists
          h[p]=0; h[q]=1
          ip=int(pos1[p]); iq=int(pos0[q])
          ones[ip]=q; pos1[q]=ip; pos1[p]=-1
          zeros[iq]=p; pos0[p]=iq; pos0[q]=-1
          if newM < bestM:
              bestM=newM; best_h=h.copy()
          curM=newM
          if no_improve > 5000: # restart? maybe cool/ randomize? 
              # do batch random? reset no_improve? 
       else:
          c -= delta
          no_improve +=1
          # if no_improve large, do random restart? within same function maybe set h to best and perturb?
       if time check...
    return best_h,bestM
```

Soft gradient computation:
```
def soft_max_and_grad(h, beta):
  n=len(h)
  c = np.empty(2n-1)
  # compute c
  off=n-1
  c[off]=np.sum(h*(1-h))
  for d in range(1,n):
     L=n-d
     c[off+d] = np.dot(h[d:], 1.0-h[:-d])
     c[off-d] = np.dot(h[:-d], 1.0-h[d:])
  m = c.max()
  z = np.exp(beta*(c-m))
  w = z / z.sum()
  grad = np.zeros(n)
  # d=0
  grad += w[off]*(1-2*h)
  for d in range(1,n):
      L=n-d
      wd = w[off+d]
      if wd > 1e-12:
          grad[:L] -= wd * h[d:]
          grad[d:n] += wd * (1 - h[:L])
      wm = w[off-d]
      if wm > 1e-12:
          # d=-m: original i=m..n-1, shifted 0..L-1
          grad[m:n] -= wm * h[:L]
          grad[:L] += wm * (1 - h[m:n])
  val = (m + np.log(z.sum()))/beta
  return val, grad, c
```
But if many weights, loop only significant. Use threshold.

        lr /=2

```

```
def project_box_sum(y, S):
   lo = min(y)-1; hi=max(y)+1
   for _ in range(60):
      t=0.5*(lo+hi); z=np.clip(y-t,0,1); s=z.sum()
      if s > S: lo=t
      else: hi=t
   t=0.5*(lo+hi); h=np.clip(y-t,0,1)
   diff = S - h.sum()
   # adjust one or more free elements
   if abs(diff)>1e-12:
      # find index with 0<h<1 and sign possible
      free = np.where((h>1e-8)&(h<1-1e-8))[0]
      if len(free): distribute equally
      else adjust one
   return np.clip(h,0,1)
```

`adjust_balanced(h,n,rng)`: Convert to uint8? If continuous, threshold top. For binary random arrays, sum adjust: If sum>W, choose random ones to zero and zeros to one until balanced. If sum<W, opposite.
```
def enforce_weight(h,n,rng):
  h=h.astype(np.uint8).copy(); W=n//2
  ones=np.flatnonzero(h); zeros=np.flatnonzero(h==0)

def get_precomp(n):
   global _PRECACHE
   if n in _PRECACHE: return _PRECACHE[n]
   off=n-1
   si=[]; so=[]; oi=[]; oo=[]
   for r in range(n):
      # shifted: i all except r
      i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32)
      mask = i != r
      iarr = i[mask]
      darr = r - iarr
      idx = off + darr
      si.append(idx); so.append(iarr)
      # original: b all except r
      barr = i[mask]
      darr2 = barr - r
      oi.append(off + darr2); oo.append(barr)
   _PRECACHE[n]=(si,so,oi,oo)
   return ...
```

Let's refine continuous_refine algorithm for reliability:
```
def continuous_refine(h0, time_limit, rng, beta=10.0):
  h0 = np.clip(np.asarray(h0,float),0,1)
  n=len(h0); S=0.5*n
  h=project_box_sum(h0,S)
  best_h=h.copy(); best_count=evaluate_max_count(h)
  lr=0.02; no_improve=0; last_improve=time.time()
  while time.time()<time_start+time_limit:
     val, grad, c = soft...; cur_count=c.max()
     if cur_count < best_count - 1e-9: update best_count,best_h,c_new? no_improve=0
     # if no_improve> iters, increase beta *=1.5 up to 100, lr*=0.7, reset no_improve
     # compute trial steps descending from lr
     improved=False
     for trial in [lr, lr*0.5, lr*0.25, lr*0.1]:
        h_new=project_box_sum(h - trial*grad, S)
        c_new=corr_shifts(h_new)
        new_count=c_new.max()
        if new_count < cur_count - 1e-8:
           h=h_new; cur_count=new_count; improved=True; lr=min(lr*1.1,0.1); break
     if not improved:
        no_improve+=1
        if no_improve>30:
           beta=min(beta*1.5,100); lr*=0.7; no_improve=0
        if lr<1e-4: break
  return best_h, best_count*2/n
```

```
def enforce_weight_binary(h,W,rng):
  h=h.astype(np.uint8).copy()
  s=int(h.sum())
  while s>W:
     ones=np.flatnonzero(h)
     p=ones[int(rng.integers(len(ones)))]
     h[p]=0; s-=1
  while s<W:
     zeros=np.flatnonzero(h==0)
     p=zeros[int(rng.integers(len(zeros)))]
     h[p]=1; s+=1
  return h
```
This can be O(k n) but k small. For random seed. Good.

import numpy as np
import time, math
_PRECACHE = {}

def evaluate_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0: return 1.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / n))
```
For n=80, np.correlate returns negative? Products nonnegative so okay.

`corr_shifts`:
```
def corr_shifts(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float); n=len(h); out=np.zeros(2*n-1,float); off=n-1
    out[off]=float(np.dot(h,1-h))
    for d in range(1,n):
        L=n-d
        out[off+d]=float(np.dot(h[d:],1-h[:-d]))
        out[off-d]=float(np.dot(h[:-d],1-h[d:]))
    return out
```
For binary, d0 zero. For continuous, d0 positive possible.

`project_box_sum`:
```
def project_box_sum(y,S):
    y=np.asarray(y,float)
    lo=float(np.min(y))-1.0; hi=float(np.max(y))+1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       t=0.5*(lo+hi); z=np.clip(y-t,0.0,1.0); s=float(z.sum())
       if s>S: lo=t
       else: hi=t
    t=0.5*(lo+hi); h=np.clip(y-t,0.0,1.0)
    diff=S-float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       free=np.where((h>1e-8)&(h<1-1e-8))[0]
       if free.size:
          cnt=min(free.size, max(1,int(abs(diff)*1e6))?)
```
Distribute: if diff positive, add diff to elements with room (1-h). We can do simple: adjust one free by diff clipped. If no free, adjust smallest room.
```
       if free.size:
          room = np.abs(S - h[free].sum())? Better:
          for idx in free:
             if diff>0:
                add=min(diff,1-h[idx]); h[idx]+=add; diff-=add
             else:
                sub=min(-diff,h[idx]); h[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub
             if abs(diff)<=1e-12: break
       return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
But loops in Python; n small. Good.
S might be float n/2; for integers, exact after binary search? Floating error small.

But for binary local, we never use projection.

`soft_max_grad`:
```
def soft_max_grad(h,beta):
   n=len(h); c=corr_shifts(h); off=n-1
   m=c.max(); z=np.exp(beta*(c-m)); w=z/z.sum(); grad=np.zeros(n,float)
   grad += w[off]*(1.0-2.0*h)
   for d in range(1,n):
      L=n-d
      wd=float(w[off+d])
      if wd>1e-10:
         grad[:L] -= wd*h[d:]
         grad[d:] += wd*(1.0-h[:L])
      wm=float(w[off-d])
      if wm>1e-10:
         grad[d:] -= wm*h[:L]  # wait d=m, original i=d..n-1, shifted 0..L-1
         grad[:L] += wm*(1.0-h[d:])
   val=(m + np.log(float(z.sum())))/beta
   return val, grad, c
```
Check d negative: m=d (positive for off-d). For c[-m] = ∑_{i=0}^{L-1} h[i](1 - h[i+m]), original i+m, shifted i. Derivative wrt h[j]: as original j (i=j-m) if j>=m: term -h_shifted => -h[j-m]. So grad[m:n] -= w * h[:L] (h[0:L] shifted). As shifted j if j<L: +1 - h[j+m] => grad[:L] += w*(1-h[m:n]). My code: `grad[d:] -= wm * h[:L]` correct; `grad[:L] += wm*(1.0-h[d:])`. Good.
For d positive: c[d] = ∑ h[i+d](1-h[i]). As original i grad[:L] -= h[d:n]? h[d:] length L. As shifted j=d..n-1 grad[d:] += 1-h[:L]. Good.

`continuous_refine`: Use c max count. Need maybe `best_c` from evaluate? We'll use `best_count` count (not C). At end C=best_count*2/n. But if c max from our corr_shifts set same as np max. Good.
```
def continuous_refine(h0, time_limit, rng, beta=10.0):
   st=time.time(); end=st+time_limit
   h=project_box_sum(np.clip(h0,0,1),0.5*len(h0))
   c=corr_shifts(h); best_count=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy()
   lr=0.04; noimp=0; beta_cur=beta; iter=0
   while time.time()<end and lr>1e-4:
      val,grad,c=soft_max_grad(h,beta_cur)
      cur=float(c.max())
      if cur < best_count - 1e-10:
         best_count=cur; best_h=h.copy(); noimp=0; iter=0
      else: noimp += 1
      improved=False
      for step in (lr,0.5*lr,0.25*lr,0.1*lr):
         hnew=project_box_sum(h-step*grad,0.5*len(h))
         cnew=corr_shifts(hnew)
         newc=float(cnew.max())
         if newc < cur - 1e-10:
            h=hnew; lr=min(0.1, lr*1.15); improved=True; break
      if not improved:
         noimp += 1
         if noimp>20:
            beta_cur=min(100.0,beta_cur*1.6); lr*=0.6; noimp=0
         if noimp>200: break
   return best_h, best_count*2.0/len(h)
```
This may be slow due `corr_shifts` loops each trial. But time limited. For n=80, c loop 79; good. For n=256, 255 loops. If 20s, maybe 100 iterations. Fine.
Potential issue: If gradient step does not reduce true max (common), noimp increases and beta/lr reduced. It might not improve. Could also use random perturbations to escape: if noimp high, add small random noise to h then project, choose if not too worse? We can implement:
```
if noimp>50: h=project_box_sum(h + 0.01*rng.normal(size=n), S); noimp=0
```
Could help.

But `soft_max_grad` uses c full every iteration. Good.

Binary local search code details. Let's draft:
```
def make_binary_state(h,n):
    hh=np.asarray(h).astype(np.int8)
    if hh.size != n: # shouldn't
    W=n//2
    s=int(hh.sum())
    # enforce maybe using random? make_binary_state doesn't have rng; assume exact.
    c=np.zeros(2*n-1,np.int64); off=n-1
    for d in range(1,n):
        L=n-d
        c[off+d]=int(np.dot(hh[d:], 1-hh[:-d]))
        c[off-d]=int(np.dot(hh[:-d], 1-hh[d:]))
    ones=[int(i) for i in np.flatnonzero(hh)]
    zeros=[int(i) for i in np.flatnonzero(1-hh)]
    pos1=np.full(n,-1,np.int64); pos0=np.full(n,-1,np.int64)
    for j,i in enumerate(ones): pos1[i]=j
    for j,i in enumerate(zeros): pos0[i]=j
    return hh,c,ones,zeros,pos1,pos0,int(c.max())
```
`np.dot(hh[d:], 1-hh[:-d])`: hh int8, 1-hh int? For d=1, dot maybe int64. Cast int. Good. If hh dtype int8 and `1-hh` produces int8? 1 is Python int, result int64 maybe. okay.

`binary_local_search` code. We'll use local variables and while.
Important: If `time_limit <=0`, return initial state best.

```
def binary_local_search(n,h0,time_limit,rng):
   if time_limit <= 0:
      hh,c,...=make_binary_state(h0,n); return hh, int(c.max())
   pre=get_precomp(n)
   si,so,oi,oo=pre
   hh,c,ones,zeros,pos1,pos0,curM=make_binary_state(h0,n)
   best_h=hh.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); bestM=curM
   delta=np.zeros(2*n-1,np.int64)
   off=n-1
   t0=time.time(); end=t0+time_limit
   noimp=0; count=0; worst=np.flatnonzero(c>=curM-1)
   rand=rng.random; randint=rng.integers
   while time.time()<end:
      count+=1
      if count & 511 ==0:
         if c.max()!=curM: curM=int(c.max()) # curM maintained
         worst=np.flatnonzero(c>=curM-1)
      if noimp>25000:
         hh,c,ones,zeros,pos1,pos0,curM=make_binary_state(best_h,n)
         noimp=0; worst=np.flatnonzero(c>=curM-1)
      frac=(time.time()-t0)/max(1e-6,end-t0)
      if frac<0.35: eq=0.85; bad={1:0.35,2:0.08,3:0.02}
      elif frac<0.75: eq=0.5; bad={1:0.2,2:0.04,3:0.01}
      else: eq=0.15; bad={1:0.08,2:0.02,3:0.0}
      p=-1; q=-1
      mode=rand()
      if mode<0.45 and worst.size:
         wi=int(worst[int(randint(worst.size))]); d=wi-off
         found=False
         if d>0:
            L=n-d
            for _ in range(10):
               i=int(randint(L)); p=i+d; q=i
               if hh[p]==1 and hh[q]==0: found=True; break
         else:
            m=-d; L=n-m
            for _ in range(10):
               i=int(randint(L)); p=i; q=i+m
               if hh[p]==1 and hh[q]==0: found=True; break
         if not found:
            p=ones[int(randint(len(ones)))]; q=zeros[int(randint(len(zeros)))]
         elif rand()<0.35:
            # one-sided
            if rand()<0.5:
               z=zeros[int(randint(len(zeros)))]
               if z != p: q = z
            else:
               o=ones[int(randint(len(ones)))]
               if o != q: p = o
      else:
         p=ones[int(randint(len(ones)))]
         q=zeros[int(randint(len(zeros)))]
      if p<0 or q<0 or p==q: continue
      delta.fill(0)
      delta[si[p]] -= (1 - hh[so[p]])
      delta[oi[p]] += hh[oo[p]]
      delta[si[q]] += (1 - hh[so[q]])
      delta[oi[q]] -= hh[oo[q]]
      delta[off + q - p] += 1
      delta[off + p - q] += 1
      c += delta
      newM=int(c.max())
      accept=False
      if newM < curM:
         accept=True; noimp=0
      elif newM == curM:
         if rand()<eq: accept=True
      else:
         prob=bad.get(newM-curM,0.0)
         if prob and rand()<prob: accept=True
      if accept:
         h swap update
         if newM < bestM:
             bestM=newM; best_h=hh.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
         curM=newM
      else:
         c -= delta
         noimp += 1
      if bestM <= 0: break # impossible? 
      # Optional target stop? no
   return best_h,bestM
```
Need ensure lists update after accepting even if equal. In `bad` dictionary constants maybe long? small (3 entries) okay. But creating dict each loop? Assign to local `bad1,bad2,bad3` to avoid. Simpler:
```
if newM - curM ==1: prob=0.3 if phase else...
```
But fine.

After accept, update `hh[p]=0; hh[q]=1` and lists. Need use integer positions. If `pos1[p]` -1 due bug? no.

ip=int(pos1[p]); iq=int(pos0[q])

zeros[iq]=p; pos0[p]=iq; pos0[q]=-1

def markov_balanced(n,p,rng):
   h=np.zeros(n,np.uint8); h[0]=int(rng.integers(2)); x=h[0]
   for i in range(1,n):
      if rng.random()<p: x=1-x
      h[i]=x
   s=int(h.sum()); W=n//2
   while s>W: ...
   while s<W: ...
   return h
```

`smooth_balanced(n,nfreq,rng)`:
```
def smooth_balanced(n,nfreq,rng):
    A=np.zeros(n,complex)
    for k in range(1,min(nfreq,n//2)+1):
        amp=1.0/math.sqrt(k)
        ph=rng.random()*2*math.pi
        A[k]=amp*complex(math.cos(ph),math.sin(ph))
        A[-k]=np.conj(A[k])
    sig=np.fft.irfft(A,n)
    sig += 1e-9*rng.random(n)
    W=n//2
    idx=np.argsort(sig)[:W]
    b=np.zeros(n,np.uint8); b[idx]=1
    return b
```
`A[-k]` for k=n/2? If n even, k up to n//2? For n=8, k=4 is Nyquist, A[-4]=A[4] same; Our loop maybe k=1..min(nfreq,n//2). For k=n/2, A[-k]=A[k], setting conj would break real? `irfft` expects only nonnegative frequencies; for k=n/2, only real imaginary should be zero? Better stop at n//2 -1 for even, or handle Nyquist real. Simpler `maxk = max(1,n//2-1)`; for odd n but n even. Use `for k in range(1, min(nfreq, n//2))` (exclude n/2). If n=2? not. For n=80 fine.
Then `np.fft.irfft(A,n)` returns real length n. Good.

`interp_parent_binary(n,parent,rng)`:
```
def parent_to_binary(n,parent,rng):
   lp=len(parent); x0=(np.arange(lp)+0.5)*(2.0/lp); x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
   y=np.interp(x,x0,parent)
   y=y+1e-9*rng.random(n)
   idx=np.argsort(y)[:n//2]
   b=np.zeros(n,np.uint8); b[idx]=1; return b
```

def make_starts(n,parent,rng):
   starts=[]
   if parent is not None:
      starts.append(parent_to_binary(n,parent,rng))
   # choose nfreq
   nf = max(2, n//32)
   starts.append(smooth_balanced(n,nf,rng))
   starts.append(markov_balanced(n,0.22,rng))
   if len(starts)<3: starts.append(random_balanced(n,rng))
   return starts
```

Need `update_best` top-level:
```
def update_best(bh,bc,bn,h,c5,n):
    if h is None: return bh,bc,bn
    c5=float(c5)
    if c5 < bc - 1e-9:
       return np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy(), c5, int(n)
    if abs(c5-bc) <= 1e-9 and int(n) < int(bn):
       return np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy(), c5, int(n)
    return bh,bc,bn
```

def project_box_sum(y,S):
    y=np.asarray(y,float)
    lo=y.min()-1; hi=y.max()+1
    for _ in range(64):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi); z=np.clip(y-mid,0,1); s=z.sum()
       if s>S: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    t=0.5*(lo+hi); h=np.clip(y-t,0,1)
    diff=S-h.sum()

          # distribute evenly? Simpler for idx in range(len(h)): if h[idx]<1 add share
       ...
```

def block_balanced(n, scale, rng):
    h=np.zeros(n,np.uint8)
    val=int(rng.integers(2)); i=0
    while i<n:

       end=i+l

`enforce_weight_binary`: For under sum, setting zeros to ones until W; for over, ones to zero. But if over by k and no zeros? n>W so zeros exist. `np.flatnonzero` each flip O(n); k may be large for random block (sum off by ~sqrt(n) ~16), okay. For smooth top exact no adjust.
```
def enforce_weight_binary(h,W,rng):
    h=h.astype(np.uint8).copy(); s=int(h.sum())
    while s>W:
       idx=np.flatnonzero(h); i=int(idx[int(rng.integers(len(idx)))]); h[i]=0; s-=1
    while s<W:
       idx=np.flatnonzero(h==0); i=int(idx[int(rng.integers(len(idx)))]); h[i]=1; s+=1
    return h
```

Let's design `pair_refine`:
```
def pair_refine(h0,time_limit,rng,max_pairs_per_pair=9):
   h=np.asarray(h0,float).copy(); n=len(h); h=project_box_sum(h,0.5*n)
   cur=float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')))
   best=h.copy(); best_count=cur
   t0=time.time(); end=t0+time_limit; noimp=0
   while time.time()<end:
      i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
      if i==j or abs(h[i]-h[j])<1e-4: 
         # still try small? choose one more
         j=int(rng.integers(n));
      maxp=min(h[i],1-h[j]); maxq=min(h[j],1-h[i])
      tested=False; improved=False
      for a in fractions: # amounts moving from i to j? We can create candidate array for delta = a (positive i->j)
         a1=a*maxp; a2=a*maxq? Need both directions.
      # Better list of deltas: [0.2,0.5,0.9]* -maxq and +maxp (move from i to j positive)
      deltas = []
      for f in (0.2,0.5,0.8): deltas.append(f*maxp) # h[i]-=a,h[j]+=a
      for f in (0.2,0.5,0.8): deltas.append(-f*maxq) # h[i]+=a,h[j]-=a
      # also tiny
      for a in deltas:
         if a==0: continue
         nh=h.copy(); nh[i]-=a; nh[j]+=a
         cmax=float(np.max(np.correlate(nh,1-nh,mode='full')))
         if cmax < cur - 1e-9:
             h=nh; cur=cmax; if cur<best_count: best...; noimp=0; improved=True; break
      if improved: continue
      noimp+=1
      if noimp>1000:
         # random perturbation small preserving sum: pick two, swap small
         h=project_box_sum(h + (rng.random(n)-0.5)*0.02, 0.5*n)
         cur=max correlate; noimp=0
      if noimp>100000: break
   return best, best_count*2/n
```
Evaluating copies each delta heavy. We can update in place: modify h[i],h[j], compute correlate, then revert. But correlate reads h; we can modify h and revert for trials. To avoid copy:
```
old_i=h[i]; old_j=h[j]
for a in deltas:
  h[i]=old_i-a; h[j]=old_j+a
  cmax=eval_count(h)
  if accept: keep; break
after trials if not accept: h[i]=old_i; h[j]=old_j
```
But if accept, h changed; new cur. Need then maybe continue? If no accept revert. Good.

Let's write `continuous_pair_refine` (name `refine_continuous`):
```
def refine_continuous(h0, time_limit, rng):
    h=np.asarray(h0,float).copy(); n=len(h); S=0.5*n
    h=project_box_sum(h,S)
    cur=float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')))
    best=h.copy(); best_count=cur
    t0=time.time(); end=t0+time_limit; noimp=0; it=0
    fracs=(0.2,0.5,0.8,0.95)
    while time.time()<end:
       it +=1
       if it % 2000 == 0? no
       i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
       if i==j: continue
       hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]
       maxp=min(hi,1.0-hj)
       maxq=min(hj,1.0-hi)
       accepted=False
       # try both directions, maybe sorted by magnitude? 
       deltas=[]
       if maxp>1e-6:
          for f in fracs: deltas.append(f*maxp)
       if maxq>1e-6:
          for f in fracs: deltas.append(-f*maxq)
       # If no deltas (both equal), tiny perturbation? choose i,j random until diff.
       for a in deltas:
          h[i]=hi-a; h[j]=hj+a
          c=float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')))
          if c < cur - 1e-9:
             cur=c; accepted=True
             if c < best_count - 1e-9:
                best_count=c; best=h.copy()
             break
       if accepted:
          noimp=0
       else:
          h[i]=hi; h[j]=hj
          noimp +=1
          if noimp > 2000:
             # perturb two random by small preserving sum
             i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
             if i!=j:
                a=0.01*(rng.random()-0.5)*2? Actually transfer amount.
                a=0.02*rng.random()
                if h[i] < a: a=0
                h[i]-=a; h[j]+=a
             h=project_box_sum(h,S)
             cur=float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')))
             # if perturbed worsened, that's okay; best retained
             noimp=0
             if it > 100000: break
       if cur < 0.0: break
    return best, best_count*(2.0/n)
```

Now, code for `binary_local_search` with local aliases. Let's write carefully.

```
def binary_local_search(n,h0,time_limit,rng):
    if time_limit <= 0:
        hh,_,_,_,_,_,m=make_binary_state(h0,n)
        return hh,m
    pre=get_precomp(n)
    si,so,oi,oo=pre
    hh,c,ones,zeros,pos1,pos0,curM=make_binary_state(h0,n)
    best_h=hh.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); bestM=curM
    delta=np.zeros(2*n-1,dtype=np.int64)
    off=n-1
    t0=time.time(); end=t0+time_limit
    worst=np.flatnonzero(c >= curM - 1)
    noimp=0; count=0
    rand=rng.random; randint=rng.integers
    while time.time() < end:
        count += 1
        if (count & 1023) == 0:
            worst=np.flatnonzero(c >= curM - 1)
        if noimp > 30000:
            hh,c,ones,zeros,pos1,pos0,curM=make_binary_state(best_h,n)
            noimp=0; worst=np.flatnonzero(c >= curM - 1)
        frac=(time.time()-t0)/(end-t0 if end>t0 else 1.0)
        if frac < 0.35:
            eq=0.85; p1=0.4; p2=0.1; p3=0.03
        elif frac < 0.75:
            eq=0.5; p1=0.25; p2=0.06; p3=0.015
        else:
            eq=0.12; p1=0.1; p2=0.02; p3=0.0
        p=-1; q=-1
        mode=float(rand())
        if mode < 0.45 and worst.size > 0:
            wi=int(worst[int(randint(worst.size))]); d=wi-off
            found=False
            if d > 0:
                L=n-d
                for _ in range(8):
                    i=int(randint(L)); p=i+d; q=i
                    if hh[p] == 1 and hh[q] == 0:
                        found=True; break
            else:
                m=-d; L=n-m
                for _ in range(8):
                    i=int(randint(L)); p=i; q=i+m
                    if hh[p] == 1 and hh[q] == 0:
                        found=True; break
            if found and float(rand()) < 0.35:
                if float(rand()) < 0.5:
                    z=int(zeros[int(randint(len(zeros)))]);
                    if z != p: q=z
                else:
                    o=int(ones[int(randint(len(ones)))]);
                    if o != q: p=o
            if not found:
                p=int(ones[int(randint(len(ones)))]); q=int(zeros[int(randint(len(zeros)))])
        else:
            p=int(ones[int(randint(len(ones)))]); q=int(zeros[int(randint(len(zeros)))])
        if p < 0 or q < 0 or p == q:
            continue
        delta.fill(0)
        delta[si[p]] -= (1 - hh[so[p]])
        delta[oi[p]] += hh[oo[p]]
        delta[si[q]] += (1 - hh[so[q]])
        delta[oi[q]] -= hh[oo[q]]
        delta[off + q - p] += 1
        delta[off + p - q] += 1
        c += delta
        newM = int(c.max())
        accept=False
        if newM < curM:
            accept=True; noimp=0
        elif newM == curM:
            if float(rand()) < eq: accept=True
        else:
            inc=newM-curM
            prob = p1 if inc==1 else (p2 if inc==2 else (p3 if inc==3 else 0.0))
            if prob > 0.0 and float(rand()) < prob: accept=True
        if accept:
            ip=int(pos1[p]); iq=int(pos0[q])
            ones[ip]=q; pos1[q]=ip; pos1[p]=-1
            zeros[iq]=p; pos0[p]=iq; pos0[q]=-1
            hh[p]=0; hh[q]=1
            curM=newM
            if newM < bestM:
                bestM=newM; best_h=hh.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
        else:
            c -= delta
            noimp += 1
    return best_h,bestM
```
Note: In one-sided if replace q=z, z is zero; if z==p impossible because p one. p=o one; if o==q impossible. Good.

But if `mode` direct found False, we fallback random. If found True but one-sided changes q/p to random, we might lose worst contribution but okay.

Potential bug: If direct found but then `rand()<0.35` and replace q with random zero, the p may no longer be a one? p still one. q new zero. Good. If replace p with random one, q still zero.

`worst` may include lag with c=0? If curM=0, not. If d=0? c0=0, not max if W>0. If worst includes d=0? No.

After reset from best_h, if `best_h` sum exact. good.

Can `noimp` counter reset on equal accept? We reset noimp=0 only on improvement. If many equal accepted, noimp not increment? Actually if accept equal, noimp remains previous. Good; plateau exploration continues. If reject, increment. If no improvement for 30000 rejections, reset. If equal accepted many, no reset but time passes. Fine.

`best_c` not used except reset? We use make_binary_state from best_h, so best_c unnecessary. But we store; okay.

def refine_continuous(h0,time_limit,rng):
   h=np.asarray(h0,float).copy()

   if n==0: return h,1.0

   best=h.copy(); best_count=cur
   t0=time.time(); end=t0+time_limit
   noimp=0; it=0
   fracs=(0.25,0.6,1.0)
   while time.time()<end:
      it +=1
      i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
      if i==j: continue
      hi=float(h[i]); hj=float(h[j])
      maxp=min(hi,1.0-hj); maxq=min(hj,1.0-hi)
      if maxp <= 1e-10 and maxq <= 1e-10: continue
      best_a=0.0; best_c=cur

      h[i]=hi; h[j]=hj

            else:

```python
import numpy as np
import time

_PRECACHE = {}

def max_count_corr(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")))

def evaluate_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return max_count_corr(h) * (2.0 / h.size) if h.size else 1.0

`project_box_sum`:
```
def project_box_sum(y, S):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    if y.size == 0:
        return y
    lo = float(y.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(y.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        t = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        z = np.clip(y - t, 0.0, 1.0)
        if float(z.sum()) > S:
            lo = t
        else:
            hi = t
    h = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = S - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        free = np.where((h > 1e-10) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-10))[0]
        for idx in free:
            if diff > 0:
                add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
                h[idx] += add
                diff -= add
            else:
                sub = min(-diff, h[idx])
                h[idx] -= sub
                diff += sub
            if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
                break
        if abs(diff) > 1e-9:
            h = np.clip(h + diff / h.size, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```
If no free and all 0/1 but sum off by tiny, adjust average may break box/sum slightly; but only tiny. If sum off large due S infeasible? S between 0,n. okay.

`enforce_weight_binary` as above.

`get_precomp`:
```
def get_precomp(n):
    if n in _PRECACHE: return _PRECACHE[n]
    off=n-1
    base=np.arange(n,dtype=np.int32)
    si=[]; so=[]; oi=[]; oo=[]
    for r in range(n):
        mask = base != r
        iarr = base[mask]
        si.append((off + r - iarr).astype(np.int32))
        so.append(iarr.copy())
        barr = iarr
        oi.append((off + barr - r).astype(np.int32))
        oo.append(barr.copy())
    _PRECACHE[n]=(si,so,oi,oo)
    return _PRECACHE[n]
```
Note `off+r-iarr` where off Python int, arrays int32 => int32? Maybe int64? Cast. Good.

`make_binary_state`:
```
def make_binary_state(h,n):
   hh=np.asarray(h).astype(np.int8).copy()
   # if hh.size != n? interpolate? assume
   W=n//2
   s=int(hh.sum())
   # quick enforce? no rng? We can enforce by flipping first differences without rng to preserve? But if start invalid and no rng. We assume valid. For safety, do deterministic adjust:
   if s > W:
       for i in range(n):
           if s==W: break
           if hh[i]==1: hh[i]=0; s-=1
   elif s < W:
       for i in range(n):
           if s==W: break
           if hh[i]==0: hh[i]=1; s+=1
   h64=hh.astype(np.int64)
   c=np.zeros(2*n-1,dtype=np.int64)
   off=n-1
   for d in range(1,n):
       c[off+d]=int(np.dot(h64[d:], 1 - h64[:-d]))
       c[off-d]=int(np.dot(h64[:-d], 1 - h64[d:]))
   ones=[int(i) for i in np.flatnonzero(hh)]
   zeros=[int(i) for i in np.flatnonzero(1 - hh)]
   pos1=np.full(n,-1,dtype=np.int64)
   pos0=np.full(n,-1,dtype=np.int64)
   for j,i in enumerate(ones): pos1[i]=j
   for j,i in enumerate(zeros): pos0[i]=j
   return hh,c,ones,zeros,pos1,pos0,int(c.max())
```
Deterministic adjust may make arbitrary start if invalid; okay.

```

def parent_continuous(n,parent):
    lp=parent.size
    x0=(np.arange(lp)+0.5)*(2.0/lp)
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    y=np.interp(x,x0,parent)
    return project_box_sum(y,0.5*n)
```

`update_best`:
```
def update_best(bh,bc,bn,h,c5,n):
    if h is None: return bh,bc,bn
    c5=float(c5)
    if bc == float('inf') or c5 < bc - 1e-10:
       return np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy(), c5, int(n)
    if abs(c5-bc) <= 1e-10 and bn > 0 and int(n) < int(bn):
       return np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy(), c5, int(n)
    return bh,bc,bn
```

Let's write `refine_continuous` final:
```
def refine_continuous(h0, time_limit, rng):
    h = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float).copy()
    n = h.size
    if n == 0 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return h, 1.0
    S = 0.5 * n
    h = project_box_sum(h, S)
    cur = max_count_corr(h)
    best = h.copy(); best_count = cur
    t0 = time.time(); end = t0 + float(time_limit)
    fracs = (0.3, 0.7, 1.0) if n <= 160 else (0.5, 1.0)
    noimp = 0; it = 0
    while time.time() < end:
        if cur > best_count + 3.0:
            h = best.copy(); cur = best_count; noimp = 0
        it += 1
        i = int(rng.integers(n)); j = int(rng.integers(n))
        if i == j:
            continue
        hi = float(h[i]); hj = float(h[j])
        maxp = min(hi, 1.0 - hj); maxq = min(hj, 1.0 - hi)
        if maxp <= 1e-10 and maxq <= 1e-10:
            continue
        best_a = 0.0; best_c = cur
        for f in fracs:
            if maxp > 1e-10:
                a = f * maxp
                h[i] = hi - a; h[j] = hj + a
                c = max_count_corr(h)
                if c < best_c: best_c = c; best_a = a
            if maxq > 1e-10:
                a = f * maxq
                h[i] = hi + a; h[j] = hj - a
                c = max_count_corr(h)
                if c < best_c: best_c = c; best_a = -a
        h[i] = hi; h[j] = hj
        if best_a != 0.0 and best_c < cur - 1e-9:
            h[i] = hi - best_a; h[j] = hj + best_a
            cur = best_c; noimp = 0
            if cur < best_count - 1e-9:
                best_count = cur; best = h.copy()
        else:
            noimp += 1
            if noimp > 2500:
                k1 = int(rng.integers(n)); k2 = int(rng.integers(n))
                if k1 != k2:
                    a = 0.02 * float(rng.random())
                    a = min(a, float(h[k1]), 1.0 - float(h[k2]))
                    h[k1] -= a; h[k2] += a
                h = project_box_sum(h, S)
                cur = max_count_corr(h)
                if cur > best_count + 2.0:
                    h = best.copy(); cur = best_count
                noimp = 0
                if it > 200000:
                    break
    return best, best_count * (2.0 / n)
```
Potential issue: In loop, for each f we do positive and negative. If maxp positive and maxq positive, we evaluate 6 candidates. Good. But if best_c improves with positive a, then next negative uses hi/hj; h temporarily modified but re-set for each candidate? For positive: h[i]=hi-a; h[j]=hj+a. For negative immediately after, h[i]=hi+a; h[j]=hj-a. No need revert between because overwritten. After all, revert. Good.
If only maxq positive (hi small, hj large), positive maxp maybe 0; negative moves increase i decrease j. Good.

Now, `run` final. Let's write.
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100.0, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.time()
    t_end = t_start + float(budget_s) - 2.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent = None
    gv = globals()
    if "initial_h_values" in gv:
        parr = np.asarray(gv["initial_h_values"], dtype=float).ravel()
        if parr.size > 0:
            parent = np.clip(parr, 0.0, 1.0)
            parent = project_box_sum(parent, 0.5 * parent.size)
    best_h = None; best_c = float("inf"); best_n = 0
    if parent is not None:
        c5p = evaluate_c5(parent)
        best_h, best_c, best_n = update_best(best_h, best_c, best_n, parent, c5p, parent.size)
    plan_frac = {80: 0.20, 128: 0.20, 160: 0.26, 256: 0.22}
    if parent is not None:
        npar = int(parent.size)
        plan_frac[npar] = plan_frac.get(npar, 0.0) + 0.10
    total_frac = sum(plan_frac.values())
    reserve = min(140.0, 0.12 * float(budget_s))
    main_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0 - reserve)
    for n, frac in sorted(plan_frac.items()):
        if n <= 0: continue
        tplan = (frac / total_frac) * main_budget
        tplan = min(tplan, t_end - time.time() - 5.0)
        if tplan < 4.0:
            continue
        plan_h = None; plan_c = float("inf")
        if parent is not None:
            try:
                hpar = parent_continuous(n, parent)
                cpar = evaluate_c5(hpar)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = update_best(best_h, best_c, best_n, hpar, cpar, n)
                if cpar < plan_c:
                    plan_h = hpar; plan_c = cpar
            except Exception:
                pass
        if n % 2 == 0 and tplan > 6.0:
            tc_reserve = min(40.0 if n <= 128 else 20.0, 0.22 * tplan)
            tbin = max(0.0, tplan - tc_reserve)
            starts = make_starts(n, parent, rng)
            if len(starts) == 3: shares = (0.5, 0.3, 0.2)
            elif len(starts) == 2: shares = (0.6, 0.4)
            else: shares = (1.0,)
            for idx, h0 in enumerate(starts):
                ts = tbin * shares[idx]
                if ts < 1.0 or time.time() > t_end - 5.0:
                    break
                bh, bm = binary_local_search(n, h0, ts, rng)
                c5b = (2.0 * float(bm)) / float(n)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = update_best(best_h, best_c, best_n, bh, c5b, n)
                if c5b < plan_c:
                    plan_h = bh; plan_c = c5b
        if plan_h is not None and tplan > 8.0:
            tc = min(40.0 if best_n <= 128 else 20.0, tplan * 0.3, t_end - time.time() - 4.0)
            if tc > 2.0:
                try:
                    hc, c5c = refine_continuous(plan_h, tc, rng)
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = update_best(best_h, best_c, best_n, hc, c5c, n)
                except Exception:
                    pass
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < t_end - 12.0:
        tc = min(120.0, t_end - time.time() - 4.0)
        try:
            hc, c5c = refine_continuous(best_h, tc, rng)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = update_best(best_h, best_c, best_n, hc, c5c, best_n)
        except Exception:
            pass
    if best_h is None:
        n = 80
        best_h = random_balanced(n, rng).astype(float)
        best_c = evaluate_c5(best_h)
        best_n = n
    else:
        best_h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float)
        if best_h.size > 0:
            best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, 0.5 * best_h.size)
            c5f = evaluate_c5(best_h)
            if c5f < best_c:
                best_c = c5f
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
Potential: `tc = min(40 if n <= 128 else 20, tplan*0.3, remaining)` uses n from plan. For final refine uses best_n. Good.

import numpy as np, time, math
_PRE={}
def mcc(h):
 h=np.asarray(h,float); return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')))
def eval5(h):
 h=np.asarray(h,float); return mcc(h)*2.0/h.size if h.size else 1.0
def proj(y,S):
 y=np.asarray(y,float); 
 if y.size==0: return y
 lo=float(y.min())-1; hi=float(y.max())+1
 for _ in range(60):
  t=.5*(lo+hi); z=np.clip(y-t,0,1)
  if z.sum()>S: lo=t
  else: hi=t
 h=np.clip(y-.5*(lo+hi),0,1); d=S-h.sum()
 if abs(d)>1e-12:
  fr=np.where((h>1e-10)&(h<1-1e-10))[0]
  for i in fr:
   if d>0:
    a=min(d,1-h[i]); h[i]+=a; d-=a
   else:
    a=min(-d,h[i]); h[i]-=a; d+=a
   if abs(d)<1e-12: break
  if abs(d)>1e-9: h=np.clip(h+d/h.size,0,1)
 return h

def enforcew(h,W,rng):
 h=np.asarray(h,np.uint8).copy(); s=int(h.sum())
 while s>W:
  i=np.flatnonzero(h)[int(rng.integers(int(np.count_nonzero(h))))]; h[i]=0; s-=1
 while s<W:
  i=np.flatnonzero(h==0)[int(rng.integers(int(np.count_nonzero(h==0))))]; h[i]=1; s+=1
 return h

def enforcew(h,W,rng):
 h=np.asarray(h,np.uint8).copy(); s=int(h.sum())
 while s!=W:
  if s>W:
   idx=np.flatnonzero(h); i=idx[int(rng.integers(idx.size))]; h[i]=0; s-=1
  else:
   idx=np.flatnonzero(h==0); i=idx[int(rng.integers(idx.size))]; h[i]=1; s+=1

get_precomp compact:
```
def prev(n):
 if n in _PRE: return _PRE[n]
 off=n-1; b=np.arange(n,dtype=np.int32); si=[]; so=[]; oi=[]; oo=[]
 for r in range(n):
  i=b[b!=r]
  si.append((off+r-i).astype(np.int32)); so.append(i.copy())
  oi.append((off+i-r).astype(np.int32)); oo.append(i.copy())
 _PRE[n]=(si,so,oi,oo); return _PRE[n]
```
Good.

make_state compact:
```
def bstate(h,n):
 hh=np.asarray(h,np.int8).copy(); W=n//2; s=int(hh.sum())
 if s>W:
  for i in range(n):
   if s==W: break
   if hh[i]: hh[i]=0; s-=1
 elif s<W:
  for i in range(n):
   if s==W: break
   if not hh[i]: hh[i]=1; s+=1
 h64=hh.astype(np.int64); c=np.zeros(2*n-1,np.int64); off=n-1
 for d in range(1,n):
  c[off+d]=int(np.dot(h64[d:],1-h64[:-d])); c[off-d]=int(np.dot(h64[:-d],1-h64[d:]))
 ones=list(np.flatnonzero(hh).astype(int)); zeros=list(np.flatnonzero(1-hh).astype(int))
 pos1=np.full(n,-1,np.int64); pos0=np.full(n,-1,np.int64)
 for j,i in enumerate(ones): pos1[i]=j
 for j,i in enumerate(zeros): pos0[i]=j
 return hh,c,ones,zeros,pos1,pos0,int(c.max())
```
20 lines.

Initial starts compact:
```
def randb(n,rng):
 i=rng.permutation(n); b=np.zeros(n,np.uint8); b[i[:n//2]]=1; return b
def smoothb(n,nf,rng):
 A=np.zeros(n,complex); mk=max(1,min(nf,n//2-1)); W=n//2
 for k in range(1,mk+1):
  p=2*math.pi*rng.random(); A[k]=math.cos(p)+1j*math.sin(p); A[-k]=math.cos(p)-1j*math.sin(p)
 s=np.fft.irfft(A,n)+1e-9*rng.random(n); b=np.zeros(n,np.uint8); b[np.argsort(s)[:W]]=1; return b
def blockb(n,sc,rng):
 h=np.zeros(n,np.uint8); v=int(rng.integers(2)); i=0
 while i<n:
  l=max(1,int(rng.exponential(max(4.0,sc)))); e=min(n,i+l); h[i:e]=v; i=e
  if i<n: v^=1
 return enforcew(h,n//2,rng)
def pbin(n,p,rng):
 lp=p.size; x0=(np.arange(lp)+.5)*2/lp; x=(np.arange(n)+.5)*2/n
 y=np.interp(x,x0,p)+1e-9*rng.random(n); b=np.zeros(n,np.uint8); b[np.argsort(y)[:n//2]]=1; return b
def starts(n,parent,rng):
 st=[]
 if parent is not None: st.append(pbin(n,parent,rng))
 else: st.append(randb(n,rng))
 st.append(smoothb(n,max(2,n//16),rng))
 st.append(blockb(n,max(6.0,n//10.0),rng))
 return st
```
~30 lines. `smoothb` uses A[-k] for k up to n//2-1 valid. Good.

`parcont`:
```
def parcont(n,p):
 lp=p.size; x0=(np.arange(lp)+.5)*2/lp; x=(np.arange(n)+.5)*2/n
 return proj(np.interp(x,x0,p),.5*n)
```

`update` compact:
```
def upd(bh,bc,bn,h,c,n):
 if h is None: return bh,bc,bn
 c=float(c)
 if bc==float('inf') or c<bc-1e-10: return np.asarray(h,float).copy(),c,int(n)
 if abs(c-bc)<=1e-10 and bn>0 and n<bn: return np.asarray(h,float).copy(),c,int(n)
 return bh,bc,bn
```

Let's write binsearch compact:
```
def binsearch(n,h0,tl,rng):
 if tl<=0:
  h,_,_,_,_,_,m=bstate(h0,n); return h,m
 si,so,oi,oo=prev(n); hh,c,ones,zeros,p1,p0,cur=bstate(h0,n)
 best=hh.copy(); bestm=cur; dl=np.zeros(2*n-1,np.int64); off=n-1
 t0=time.time(); end=t0+tl; worst=np.flatnonzero(c>=cur-1); no=0; cnt=0
 rand=rng.random; ri=rng.integers
 while time.time()<end:
  cnt+=1
  if cnt&1023: worst=np.flatnonzero(c>=cur-1)
  if no>30000:
   hh,c,ones,zeros,p1,p0,cur=bstate(best,n); no=0; worst=np.flatnonzero(c>=cur-1)
  f=(time.time()-t0)/(end-t0 if end>t0 else 1.0)
  if f<.35: eq,b1,b2,b3=.85,.4,.1,.03
  elif f<.75: eq,b1,b2,b3=.5,.25,.06,.015
  else: eq,b1,b2,b3=.12,.1,.02,0
  p=q=-1; m=rand()
  if m<.45 and worst.size:
   w=int(worst[int(ri(worst.size))]); d=w-off; ok=False
   if d>0:
    L=n-d
    for _ in range(8):
     i=int(ri(L)); p=i+d; q=i
     if hh[p]==1 and hh[q]==0: ok=True; break
   else:
    L=n+d # since d negative? if d=-m, L=n-|d|=n+d.
    for _ in range(8):
     i=int(ri(L)); p=i; q=i-d
     if hh[p]==1 and hh[q]==0: ok=True; break
   if ok and rand()<.35:
    if rand()<.5:
     z=zeros[int(ri(len(zeros)))];
     if z!=p: q=z
    else:
     o=ones[int(ri(len(ones)))];
     if o!=q: p=o
   if not ok:
    p=ones[int(ri(len(ones)))]; q=zeros[int(ri(len(zeros)))]
  else:
   p=ones[int(ri(len(ones)))]; q=zeros[int(ri(len(zeros)))]
  if p<0 or q<0 or p==q: continue
  dl.fill(0)
  dl[si[p]] -= 1 - hh[so[p]]
  dl[oi[p]] += hh[oo[p]]
  dl[si[q]] += 1 - hh[so[q]]
  dl[oi[q]] -= hh[oo[q]]
  dl[off+q-p] += 1; dl[off+p-q] += 1
  c += dl; newm=int(c.max()); acc=False
  if newm<cur: acc=True; no=0
  elif newm==cur: acc=rand()<eq
  else:
   inc=newm-cur; pr=b1 if inc==1 else b2 if inc==2 else b3 if inc==3 else 0
   if pr and rand()<pr: acc=True
  if acc:
   ip=p1[p]; iq=p0[q]
   ones[ip]=q; p1[q]=ip; p1[p]=-1
   zeros[iq]=p; p0[p]=iq; p0[q]=-1
   hh[p]=0; hh[q]=1; cur=newm
   if newm<bestm: bestm=newm; best=hh.copy()
  else:
   c -= dl; no += 1
 return best,bestm
```

`refine` compact ~50. Let's write compact:
```
def refine(h0,tl,rng):
 h=np.asarray(h0,float).copy(); n=h.size
 if n==0 or tl<=0: return h,1.0
 S=.5*n; h=proj(h,S); cur=mcc(h); best=h.copy(); bc=cur
 t0=time.time(); end=t0+tl; fr=(0.3,0.7,1.0) if n<=160 else (0.5,1.0); no=0; it=0
 while time.time()<end:
  if cur>bc+3: h=best.copy(); cur=bc; no=0
  it+=1; i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
  if i==j: continue
  hi=float(h[i]); hj=float(h[j]); mp=min(hi,1-hj); mq=min(hj,1-hi)
  if mp<=1e-10 and mq<=1e-10: continue
  ba=0.0; bcc=cur
  for fr_ in fr:
   if mp>1e-10:
    a=fr_*mp; h[i]=hi-a; h[j]=hj+a; cv=mcc(h)
    if cv<bcc: bcc=cv; ba=a
   if mq>1e-10:
    a=fr_*mq; h[i]=hi+a; h[j]=hj-a; cv=mcc(h)
    if cv<bcc: bcc=cv; ba=-a
  h[i]=hi; h[j]=hj
  if ba and bcc<cur-1e-9:
   h[i]=hi-ba; h[j]=hj+ba; cur=bcc; no=0
   if cur<bc-1e-9: bc=cur; best=h.copy()
  else:
   no+=1
   if no>2500:
    k1=int(rng.integers(n)); k2=int(rng.integers(n))
    if k1!=k2:
     a=min(0.02*float(rng.random()), float(h[k1]), 1-float(h[k2])); h[k1]-=a; h[k2]+=a
    h=proj(h,S); cur=mcc(h)
    if cur>bc+2: h=best.copy(); cur=bc
    no=0
    if it>200000: break
 return best, bc*2.0/n
```
~35 lines. Good.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100.0,**kw):
 ts=time.time(); te=ts+budget_s-2; rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
 parent=None; gv=globals()
 if 'initial_h_values' in gv:
  p=np.asarray(gv['initial_h_values'],float).ravel()
  if p.size: parent=proj(np.clip(p,0,1),.5*p.size)
 bh,bc,bn=None,float('inf'),0
 if parent is not None: bh,bc,bn=upd(bh,bc,bn,parent,eval5(parent),parent.size)
 pf={80:.20,128:.20,160:.26,256:.22}
 if parent is not None: pf[int(parent.size)]=pf.get(int(parent.size),0.0)+.10
 tf=sum(pf.values()); main=max(1.0,budget_s-8-min(140,.12*budget_s))
 for n,fr in sorted(pf.items()):
  if n<=0: continue
  tp=min(fr/tf*main, te-time.time()-5)
  if tp<4: continue
  ph=None; pc=float('inf')
  if parent is not None:
   try:
    hp=parcont(n,parent); cp=eval5(hp)
    bh,bc,bn=upd(bh,bc,bn,hp,cp,n)
    if cp<pc: ph,hp? wait ph=hp; pc=cp
   except: pass
  if n%2==0 and tp>6:
   tc=min(40 if n<=128 else 20, .22*tp); tb=max(0,tp-tc)
   st=starts(n,parent,rng); sh=(.5,.3,.2) if len(st)==3 else (.6,.4) if len(st)==2 else (1,)
   for idx,h0 in enumerate(st):
    tstartb=tb*sh[idx]
    if tstartb<1 or time.time()>te-5: break
    hbb,bm=binsearch(n,h0,tstartb,rng)
    cb=2*bm/n
    bh,bc,bn=upd(bh,bc,bn,hbb,cb,n)
    if cb<pc: ph=hbb; pc=cb
  if ph is not None and tp>8:
   tc=min(40 if ph.size<=128 else 20, .3*tp, te-time.time()-4)
   if tc>2:
    try:
     hc,cc=refine(ph,tc,rng); bh,bc,bn=upd(bh,bc,bn,hc,cc,n)
    except: pass
 if bh is not None and time.time()<te-12:
  tc=min(120, te-time.time()-4); hc,cc=refine(bh,tc,rng); bh,bc,bn=upd(bh,bc,bn,hc,cc,bn)
 if bh is None:
  bn=80; bh=randb(bn,rng).astype(float); bc=eval5(bh)
 else:
  bh=np.asarray(bh,float)
  if bh.size:
   bh=proj(bh,.5*bh.size)
   cf=eval5(bh)
   if cf<bc: bc=cf
 return bh,float(bc),int(bn)

def smoothb(n,nf,rng):
 m=n//2+1; A=np.zeros(m,complex); mk=min(nf,m-1)

 s=np.fft.irfft(A,n)
```

```
def smoothb(n,nf,rng):
 m=n//2+1; A=np.zeros(m,complex); mk=min(int(nf),m-1); W=n//2
 for k in range(1,mk+1):
  A[k]=rng.normal(0,1)+1j*rng.normal(0,1)
 s=np.fft.irfft(A,n)+1e-9*rng.random(n)
 b=np.zeros(n,np.uint8); b[np.argsort(s)[:W]]=1; return b
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

_PRE = {}

def mcc(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, float)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")))

def eval5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, float)
    return mcc(h) * (2.0 / h.size) if h.size else 1.0

def proj(y, S):
    y = np.asarray(y, float)
    if y.size == 0:
        return y
    lo = float(y.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(y.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        t = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        z = np.clip(y - t, 0.0, 1.0)
        if float(z.sum()) > S:
            lo = t
        else:
            hi = t
    h = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    d = S - float(h.sum())
    if abs(d) > 1e-12:
        fr = np.where((h > 1e-10) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-10))[0]
        for i in fr:
            if d > 0:
                a = min(d, 1.0 - h[i])
                h[i] += a
                d -= a
            else:
                a = min(-d, h[i])
                h[i] -= a
                d += a
            if abs(d) < 1e-12:
                break
        if abs(d) > 1e-9:
            h = np.clip(h + d / h.size, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h

def enforcew(h, W, rng):
    h = np.asarray(h, np.uint8).copy()
    s = int(h.sum())
    while s != W:
        if s > W:
            idx = np.flatnonzero(h)
            i = idx[int(rng.integers(idx.size))]
            h[i] = 0
            s -= 1
        else:
            idx = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)
            i = idx[int(rng.integers(idx.size))]
            h[i] = 1
            s += 1
    return h

def prev(n):
    if n in _PRE:
        return _PRE[n]
    off = n - 1
    b = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32)
    si, so, oi, oo = [], [], [], []
    for r in range(n):
        i = b[b != r]
        si.append((off + r - i).astype(np.int32))
        so.append(i.copy())
        oi.append((off + i - r).astype(np.int32))
        oo.append(i.copy())
    _PRE[n] = (si, so, oi, oo)
    return _PRE[n]

def bstate(h, n):
    hh = np.asarray(h, np.int8).copy()
    W = n // 2
    s = int(hh.sum())
    if s > W:
        for i in range(n):
            if s == W:
                break
            if hh[i]:
                hh[i] = 0
                s -= 1
    elif s < W:
        for i in range(n):
            if s == W:
                break
            if not hh[i]:
                hh[i] = 1
                s += 1
    h64 = hh.astype(np.int64)
    c = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int64)
    off = n - 1
    for d in range(1, n):
        c[off + d] = int(np.dot(h64[d:], 1 - h64[:-d]))
        c[off - d] = int(np.dot(h64[:-d], 1 - h64[d:]))
    ones = [int(x) for x in np.flatnonzero(hh)]
    zeros = [int(x) for x in np.flatnonzero(1 - hh)]
    p1 = np.full(n, -1, dtype=np.int64)
    p0 = np.full(n, -1, dtype=np.int64)
    for j, i in enumerate(ones):
        p1[i] = j
    for j, i in enumerate(zeros):
        p0[i] = j
    return hh, c, ones, zeros, p1, p0, int(c.max())

def randb(n, rng):
    i = rng.permutation(n)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.uint8)
    b[i[:n // 2]] = 1
    return b

def smoothb(n, nf, rng):
    m = n // 2 + 1
    A = np.zeros(m, dtype=complex)
    mk = min(int(nf), m - 1)
    for k in range(1, mk + 1):
        A[k] = rng.normal(0, 1) + 1j * rng.normal(0, 1)
    s = np.fft.irfft(A, n) + 1e-9 * rng.random(n)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.uint8)
    b[np.argsort(s)[:n // 2]] = 1
    return b

def blockb(n, sc, rng):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.uint8)
    v = int(rng.integers(2))
    i = 0
    while i < n:
        l = max(1, int(rng.exponential(max(4.0, float(sc)))))
        e = min(n, i + l)
        h[i:e] = v
        i = e
        if i < n:
            v ^= 1
    return enforcew(h, n // 2, rng)

def pbin(n, parr, rng):
    lp = parr.size
    x0 = (np.arange(lp) + 0.5) * (2.0 / lp)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    y = np.interp(x, x0, parr) + 1e-9 * rng.random(n)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.uint8)
    b[np.argsort(y)[:n // 2]] = 1
    return b

def parcont(n, parr):
    lp = parr.size
    x0 = (np.arange(lp) + 0.5) * (2.0 / lp)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    return proj(np.interp(x, x0, parr), 0.5 * n)

def starts(n, parent, rng):
    st = []
    if parent is not None:
        st.append(pbin(n, parent, rng))
    else:
        st.append(randb(n, rng))
    st.append(smoothb(n, max(2, n // 16), rng))
    st.append(blockb(n, max(6.0, float(n) // 10.0), rng))
    return st

def upd(bh, bc, bn, h, c, n):
    if h is None:
        return bh, bc, bn
    c = float(c)
    if bc == float("inf") or c < bc - 1e-10:
        return np.asarray(h, float).copy(), c, int(n)
    if abs(c - bc) <= 1e-10 and bn > 0 and int(n) < int(bn):
        return np.asarray(h, float).copy(), c, int(n)
    return bh, bc, bn

def binsearch(n, h0, tl, rng):
    if tl <= 0:
        h, _, _, _, _, _, m = bstate(h0, n)
        return h, m
    si, so, oi, oo = prev(n)
    hh, c, ones, zeros, p1, p0, cur = bstate(h0, n)
    best = hh.copy()
    bestm = cur
    dl = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int64)
    off = n - 1
    t0 = time.time()
    end = t0 + float(tl)
    worst = np.flatnonzero(c >= cur - 1)
    no = 0
    cnt = 0
    rand = rng.random
    ri = rng.integers
    while time.time() < end:
        cnt += 1
        if (cnt & 1023) == 0:
            worst = np.flatnonzero(c >= cur - 1)
        if no > 30000:
            hh, c, ones, zeros, p1, p0, cur = bstate(best, n)
            no = 0
            worst = np.flatnonzero(c >= cur - 1)
        f = (time.time() - t0) / (end - t0 if end > t0 else 1.0)
        if f < 0.35:
            eq, b1, b2, b3 = 0.85, 0.4, 0.1, 0.03
        elif f < 0.75:
            eq, b1, b2, b3 = 0.5, 0.25, 0.06, 0.015
        else:
            eq, b1, b2, b3 = 0.12, 0.1, 0.02, 0.0
        p = q = -1
        mode = float(rand())
        if mode < 0.45 and worst.size:
            w = int(worst[int(ri(worst.size))])
            d = w - off
            ok = False
            if d > 0:
                L = n - d
                for _ in range(8):
                    i = int(ri(L))
                    p = i + d
                    q = i
                    if hh[p] == 1 and hh[q] == 0:
                        ok = True
                        break
            else:
                L = n + d
                for _ in range(8):
                    i = int(ri(L))
                    p = i
                    q = i - d
                    if hh[p] == 1 and hh[q] == 0:
                        ok = True
                        break
            if ok and float(rand()) < 0.35:
                if float(rand()) < 0.5:
                    z = zeros[int(ri(len(zeres)]) if False else int(ri(len(zzeros) if False else len(zers)))]
                    if z != p:
                        q = z
                else:
                    o = ones[int(ri(len(ones)))]
                    if o != q:
                        p = o
            if not ok:
                p = ones[int(ri(len(ones)))]
                q = zeros[int(ri(len(zeros)))]
        else:
            p = ones[int(ri(len(ones)))]
            q = zeros[int(ri(len(zeros)))]
        if p < 0 or q < 0 or p == q:
            continue
        dl.fill(0)
        dl[si[p]] -= 1 - hh[so[p]]
        dl[oi[p]] += hh[oo[p]]
        dl[si[q]] += 1 - hh[so[q]]
        dl[oi[q]] -= hh[oo[q]]
        dl[off + q - p] += 1
        dl[off + p - q] += 1
        c += dl
        newm = int(c.max())
        acc = False
        if newm < cur:
            acc = True
            no = 0
        elif newm == cur:
            if float(rand()) < eq:
                acc = True
        else:
            inc = newm - cur
            pr = b1 if inc == 1 else (b2 if inc == 2 else (b3 if inc == 3 else 0.0))
            if pr > 0.0 and float(rand()) < pr:
                acc = True
        if acc:
            ip = p1[p]
            iq = p0[q]
            ones[ip] = q
            p1[q] = ip
            p1[p] = -1
            zeros[iq] = p
            p0[p] = iq
            p0[q] = -1
            hh[p] = 0
            hh[q] = 1
            cur = newm
            if newm < bestm:
                bestm = newm
                best = hh.copy()
        else:
            c -= dl
            no += 1
    return best, bestm

def refine(h0, tl, rng):
    h = np.asarray(h0, float).copy()
    n = h.size
    if n == 0 or float(tl) <= 0.0:
        return h, 1.0
    S = 0.5 * n
    h = proj(h, S)
    cur = mcc(h)
    best = h.copy()
    bc = cur
    t0 = time.time()
    end = t0 + float(tl)
    fr = (0.3, 0.7, 1.0) if n <= 160 else (0.5, 1.0)
    no = 0
    it = 0
    while time.time() < end:
        if cur > bc + 3.0:
            h = best.copy()
            cur = bc
            no = 0
        it += 1
        i = int(rng.integers(n))
        j = int(rng.integers(n))
        if i == j:
            continue
        hi = float(h[i])
        hj = float(h[j])
        mp = min(hi, 1.0 - hj)
        mq = min(hj, 1.0 - hi)
        if mp <= 1e-10 and mq <= 1e-10:
            continue
        ba = 0.0
        bcc = cur
        for g in fr:
            if mp > 1e-10:
                a = g * mp
                h[i] = hi - a
                h[j] = hj + a
                cv = mcc(h)
                if cv < bcc:
                    bcc = cv
                    ba = a
            if mq > 1e-10:
                a = g * mq
                h[i] = hi + a
                h[j] = hj - a
                cv = mcc(h)
                if cv < bcc:
                    bcc = cv
                    ba = -a
        h[i] = hi
        h[j] = hj
        if ba != 0.0 and bcc < cur - 1e-9:
            h[i] = hi - ba
            h[j] = hj + ba
            cur = bcc
            no = 0
            if cur < bc - 1e-9:
                bc = cur
                best = h.copy()
        else:
            no += 1
            if no > 2500:
                k1 = int(rng.integers(n))
                k2 = int(rng.integers(n))
                if k1 != k2:
                    a = min(0.02 * float(rng.random()), float(h[k1]), 1.0 - float(h[k2]))
                    h[k1] -= a
                    h[k2] += a
                h = proj(h, S)
                cur = mcc(h)
                if cur > bc + 2.0:
                    h = best.copy()
                    cur = bc
                no = 0
                if it > 200000:
                    break
    return best, bc * (2.0 / n)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100.0, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.time()
    t_end = t_start + float(budget_s) - 2.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent = None
    gv = globals()
    if "initial_h_values" in gv:
        p = np.asarray(gv["initial_h_values"], float).ravel()
        if p.size:
            parent = proj(np.clip(p, 0.0, 1.0), 0.5 * p.size)
    best_h, best_c, best_n = None, float("inf"), 0
    if parent is not None:
        best_h, best_c, best_n = upd(best_h, best_c, best_n, parent, eval5(parent), parent.size)
    pf = {80: 0.20, 128: 0.20, 160: 0.26, 256: 0.22}
    if parent is not None:
        npar = int(parent.size)
        pf[npar] = pf.get(npar, 0.0) + 0.10
    tf = sum(pf.values())
    main_budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0 - min(140.0, 0.12 * float(budget_s)))
    for n, fr in sorted(pf.items()):
        if n <= 0:
            continue
        tp = min(fr / tf * main_budget, t_end - time.time() - 5.0)
        if tp < 4.0:
            continue
        ph = None
        pc = float("inf")
        if parent is not None:
            try:
                hp = parcont(n, parent)
                cp = eval5(hp)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = upd(best_h, best_c, best_n, hp, cp, n)
                if cp < pc:
                    ph = hp
                    pc = cp
            except Exception:
                pass
        if n % 2 == 0 and tp > 6.0:
            tc_res = min(40.0 if n <= 128 else 20.0, 0.22 * tp)
            tb = max(0.0, tp - tc_res)
            st = starts(n, parent, rng)
            if len(st) == 3:
                sh = (0.5, 0.3, 0.2)
            elif len(st) == 2:
                sh = (0.6, 0.4)
            else:
                sh = (1.0,)
            for idx, h0 in enumerate(st):
                ts = tb * sh[idx]
                if ts < 1.0 or time.time() > t_end - 5.0:
                    break
                bh, bm = binsearch(n, h0, ts, rng)
                cb = (2.0 * float(bm)) / float(n)
                best_h, best_c, best_n = upd(best_h, best_c, best_n, bh, cb, n)
                if cb < pc:
                    ph = bh
                    pc = cb
        if ph is not None and tp > 8.0:
            tc = min(40.0 if ph.size <= 128 else 20.0, 0.3 * tp, t_end - time.time() - 4.0)
            if tc > 2.0:
                try:
                    hc, cc = refine(ph, tc, rng)
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = upd(best_h, best_c, best_n, hc, cc, n)
                except Exception:
                    pass
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < t_end - 12.0:
        tc = min(120.0, t_end - time.time() - 4.0)
        try:
            hc, cc = refine(best_h, tc, rng)
            best_h, best_c, best_n = upd(best_h, best_c, best_n, hc, cc, best_n)
        except Exception:
            pass
    if best_h is None:
        best_n = 80
        best_h = randb(best_n, rng).astype(float)
        best_c = eval5(best_h)
    else:
        best_h = np.asarray(best_h, float)
        if best_h.size:
            best_h = proj(best_h, 0.5 * best_h.size)
            cf = eval5(best_h)
            if cf < best_c:
                best_c = cf
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
